Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the space object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 505. The moduli space has dimension at most 334 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on space structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 505. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 334. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the space structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 978. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 789 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the space decomposition. By minimality, the element a^978 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 789-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
